ENTRY       EC 4.1.2.13                 Enzyme
NAME        fructose-bisphosphate aldolase;
            aldolase;
            fructose-1,6-bisphosphate triosephosphate-lyase;
            fructose diphosphate aldolase;
            diphosphofructose aldolase;
            fructose 1,6-diphosphate aldolase;
            ketose 1-phosphate aldolase;
            phosphofructoaldolase;
            zymohexase;
            fructoaldolase;
            fructose 1-phosphate aldolase;
            fructose 1-monophosphate aldolase;
            1,6-diphosphofructose aldolase;
            SMALDO;
            D-fructose-1,6-bisphosphate D-glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate-lyase
CLASS       Lyases;
            Carbon-carbon lyases;
            Aldehyde-lyases
SYSNAME     D-fructose-1,6-bisphosphate D-glyceraldehyde-3-phosphate-lyase (glycerone-phosphate-forming)
REACTION    D-fructose 1,6-bisphosphate = glycerone phosphate + D-glyceraldehyde 3-phosphate [RN:R01068]
ALL_REAC    R01068 > R01070;
            (other) R01829 R02568
SUBSTRATE   D-fructose 1,6-bisphosphate [CPD:C00354]
PRODUCT     glycerone phosphate [CPD:C00111];
            D-glyceraldehyde 3-phosphate [CPD:C00118]
COMMENT     Also acts on (3S,4R)-ketose 1-phosphates. The yeast and bacterial enzymes are zinc proteins. The enzymes increase electron-attraction by the carbonyl group, some (Class I) forming a protonated imine with it, others (Class II), mainly of microbial origin, polarizing it with a metal ion, e.g. zinc.
HISTORY     EC 4.1.2.13 created 1965, modified 1999 (EC 4.1.2.7 created 1961, incorporated 1972)
